                                       老師說 今天只有一次，應好好把握做該做的事。



  Part 1           詞彙片語
（1）滿級分必考詞彙

absence [`Absns]          n. 缺席，不在     absent (a.) 缺席的           present（出席的）
action [`AkSEn]           n. 行動        act (v.)(n.) 行動 (n.) 法案
addition [E`dISEn]        n. 加（法）      in addition (adv.) 除此之外＝besides
advance [Ed`vAns]         n.∕v. 前進     Not to advance is to drop back. 不進則退。
affair [E`fHr]            n. 事情；戀情     interior affair (n.) 內政      foreign affair
basement [`besmEnt]       n. 地下室       be based on＋sth. (v.) 以…為基礎
basis [`besIs]            n. 基礎        on the basis of＋sth. (adv.) 根據…
battle [`bAt<]            n.∕v. 戰鬥     historic battle (n.) 史上著名的戰役
cafeteria [*kAfE`tIrIE]   n. 自助餐廳      buffet (n.) 自助餐∕gourmet (n.)美食家

 Part 2       綜合測驗
綜合測驗占分 15 分。3 篇 15 題，每篇 5 題，每題 1 分。這類題型測驗文意解讀、重要
文法、重要詞類。在這個單元，我們先了解學測方向，並複習最常考的重點：
!文意解讀：考單字、片語。請同學注意重要片語的用法，例如：
★ 1 導致         起因於
              bring about                                 come of
              result in                                   result from
              lead to                                     be due to
     原因＋      contribute to        ＋結果.         結果＋ be attributed to       ＋原因.
              give rise to                                arise from
              account for                                 occur from
              be responsible for                          stem from
     z Diligence results in success.＝Success results from diligence.     勤勞導致成功。
    2 組成…，由…組成
     分子     ＋make up ＋範圍....＝範圍＋be made up of ＋分子....
     ＝分子＋compose ＋範圍....＝範圍＋be composed of ＋分子....
     ＝分子＋constitute＋範圍....＝範圍＋consist of                       ＋分子....
     z Water is composed of hydrogen and oxygen.      水由氫和氧組成。
@重要文法：常考分詞構句、分詞片語、關係詞、時態、動名詞、不定詞、分詞。
★ 1 分詞構句
     Ving（主動）∕p.p.（被動）..., S＋V ....
     S＋V ..., Ving（主動）∕p.p.（被動）....
     z Not knowing what to do, the young man asked me for help.
        這位年輕人不知道怎麼辦，請我幫助他。
★ 2 分詞片語
     ...＋關係代名詞＋V ... ! ...＋Ving（主動）∕p.p.（被動）∕adj. 詞組 ...
     z The lady who is dressed in a red suit is the manager.
        ＝The lady dressed in a red suit is the manager.
        這位穿紅色套裝的小姐是經理。
★ 3 關係詞
     N＋關係代名詞（who(m)∕which∕that）＋不完整句
     N＋關係副詞（where∕when∕why） ＋完整句
                                                                                第 1 關      9


     z Bhutan is a country             the people are poor but happy（完整句） .
           不丹是一個人民貧窮卻快樂的國家。!空格要填 where。
 4 分詞（請注意使役動詞、keep∕leave∕find 的用法）
            make （使）                   V （主動）....
     S＋                       ＋O＋
            have （要）                   p.p. （被動）....
                              V      （主動）....                              to V （主動）....
     S＋let（讓）＋O＋                                     S＋get（要）＋O＋
                              be p.p. （被動）....                             p.p. （被動）....
            keep （讓，保持）                       Ving （主動）....
     S＋ leave （讓，任由）                 ＋O＋      p.p. （被動）....
            find （發覺）                         adj.   （狀態）....
     z We should let bygones be bygones.        過去的事，我們就該讓它過去。
#重要詞類：綜合測驗會考的詞類包括連接詞、介系詞、副詞等。
 1 if（假如）∕unless（除非）∕as long as（只要）∕as soon as（一…就…）∕
     as（當；隨著；因為；如同）∕while（當；雖然；然而）∕until（直到）∕
     by the time（到了…的時候）∕since（自從；既然）∕although（雖然）
 2 as      表示「身分」，例：As＋身分, S＋V ....（身為…）
     for   表示「原因」，例：be famous for、compete with sb. for sth.（競爭）
     with 表示「有著」、「附帶」，常作 with＋O＋Ving（主動）∕p.p.（被動）
     into 表示「結果」，例：change∕turn∕transform＋A＋into＋B（變成）
★ 3 even（甚至）∕however（然而，但是）∕therefore（因此）∕besides（此外）∕
     instead（反而）∕in contrast（對照下）∕on the contrary（相反地）∕
     likewise（同樣地）∕otherwise（否則）∕on the other hand（另一方面）




     The apple is one of the world’s most commonly cultivated fruit trees,        1     over
7,000 different kinds in existence. Despite their great variety,   2    , most apples can be
traced back to a common ancestor, the wild apple of Central Asia, Malus sieversii. They
have been grown for thousands of years.       3      the world over, apples have formed the
basis for multi-million dollar industries.                                        【r學測】
1 A as                    B for                 C into                 D with
2 A however               B instead             C otherwise            D therefore
3 A Eat                   B Eating              C Eaten                D To eat

  Part 3         文意選填
文意選填占分 10 分。1 篇 10 題，每題 1 分。本題型測驗八大詞類、五大句型。在這個
單元，我們先了解學測方向，並複習最常考的重點：
!作答時，先用鉛筆在選項旁標出「詞性」，而後才讀文章作答。
      1 注意名詞的型態：
        名詞若以 -s 或 -es 結束，表示該名詞為「可數名詞」，前方可以有 a large number
        of、a few 等修飾字，但不能有 a large amount of、a little 等。
                    修飾「可數名詞」與
必考                                               只修飾「可數名詞」 只修飾「不可數名詞」
                    「不可數名詞」均可
         幾乎沒有       almost no                    few                        little
                                                 a few                      a little
         一些         some
                                                 a number of                an amount of
                    a lot of                     many                       much
                    lots of                      not a few                  not a little
         許多         plenty of                    quite a few                quite a little
                    an abundance of              a large number of          a large amount of
                    quantities of                numerous                   a good∕great deal of
      2 注意動詞的型態：
        1 -s∕-es ! 第三人稱、單數、現在式
        2 -ing    ! 動名詞（表示一件事）∕現在分詞（表示主動、正在、令人…的）
        3 -ed     ! 過去式∕過去分詞（表示被動、已經、感到…的）
        4 to＋V ! 不定詞（表示目的、未來）
@作答時，先判讀空格的「詞性」，最後才根據「文意」答題。
#我們以 106 年學測文意選填的其中五格為例，示範答題如下：
      第一步驟：先用鉛筆在選項旁標出「詞性」。（先讀 p.11 的選項。）
      第二步驟：讀文章。先判讀空格的「詞性」，最後才根據「文意」答題。
      Ancient Egyptians admired onions. Onions were depicted in many paintings                   1
inside pyramids and tombs that span the history of ancient Egypt. They               2     as a funeral
offering shown upon the altars of the gods. The dead were buried with onions and onion
f lowers on or around various      3     of their bodies. Mummies have also been found with
onions and onion f lowers      4    their pelvis, chest, ears, eyes, and feet.
      Some scholars theorize that onions may have been used for the dead because it was
believed that their strong scent would       5     the dead to breathe again. Other researchers
                                                                                 第 1 關       11


believe it was because onions were known for their special curative properties, which would
be helpful in the afterlife.                                                       【y學測】

1     1      inside pyramids and tombs that span the history of ancient Egypt 是修飾語，修飾
    前方名詞 paintings。
    !答案為H discovered（發現）。原作... in many paintings which were discovered
      inside pyramids and tombs ... .。
2 主詞（本句的 They）後方接動詞。
    !根據文意與字詞用法，答案為D functioned（發揮功能）。
      function as＋sb.∕sth. (v.) 當作…＝act as ...＝serve as ...
3 形容詞（本句的 various，不同的；許多的）修飾名詞。
    !根據文意，答案為B parts（部位）。
4 介系詞 with 可以表示「附帶狀況」：
    with＋O＋Ving（主動）∕p.p.（被動）/ adj.∕介系詞＋N ...
    !根據文意，答案為F decorating（裝飾）。
5 助動詞（本句的would，將會）後方接原形動詞。
    !選項中，唯一的原形動詞是E prompt（促使；激起）。

     請不要考慮空格的文意，直接判斷空格的詞性，並填入正確答案。

       Strolling through streets in Taiwan, people often see a convenience store within
  a short walking distance. Taiwan is       1     first in the world for having the greatest

  Part 4          閱讀測驗
閱讀測驗占分 32 分。4 篇 16 題，每篇 4 題，每題 2 分。本題型測驗文意理解、整理、
推論等。在這個單元，我們先了解學測方向，並複習最常考的重點：
!每天至少練習一篇「閱讀測驗」，使自己具備答題的敏銳度。
@練習時，與考場答題時，都先「讀題目」，而後才讀文章。
      1 第一步驟：
         先讀題目，知道考題方向。有三種考型的答題模式極度簡易，說明如下：
必考 考型 1          主旨型的考題，例如 What is the main idea of the article?
                 答題關鍵通常在文章「每一段的第一句和最後一句」。
        考型 2     解釋型的考題，例如 According to the passage, what does the word
                 acknowledge mean?
                 答題關鍵通常在「該字所在的那一句，和前一句及後一句」。
        考型 3     推論型的考題，例如 What can we infer from the article?
                 答題關鍵通常在「全文倒數三到五行」。
      2 第二步驟：
         閱讀文章。閱讀時要整理文章；對時間、空間、人與人之間，要有清楚的思路。
#整體而言，同學在閱讀文章時，要將以下部分劃底線、圈出：
      1 各段首、尾句                               2 表示「因果關係」的句子
      3 有「承轉語詞（p.22）」的句子 4 有「比較級、最高級、強調字詞」的句子
      5 有「項目列舉」的句子                           6 有「數據、年代」的句子
      7 有「粗體字（或用引號引出的單字、片語）」的句子
$102 年學測的閱讀測驗為經典考題，示範答題如下（先讀 p.13 的題目）：
      Space is where our future is — trips to the Moon, Mars, and beyond.（首句）Most people
would think that aside from comets and stars, there is little else out there. But, since our space
journey started, we have left so much trash there that scientists are now concerned that if we
do not clean it up, we may all be in mortal danger.（尾句、承轉語詞）
      The first piece of space junk was created in 1964, when the American satellite Vanguard I
stopped operating and lost its connection with the ground center.（首句、強調字詞、年代）
However, since it kept orbiting around the Earth without any consequence, scientists became
increasingly comfortable about abandoning things that no longer served any useful purpose in
space.（尾句、承轉語詞、因果關係）
                                                                                 第 1 關      13


   The major problem with space trash is that it may hit working satellites and damage
traveling spacecraft.（首句、強調字詞） Pieces of junk may also collide with each other
and break into fragments which fall back to the Earth. To avoid this, scientists have devised
several ways to clear the sky（列舉 1）. Ground stations have been built to monitor larger
pieces of space trash（列舉 2） to prevent them from crashing into working satellites or space
shuttles. Future plans include cooperative efforts among many nations to stop littering in space
and to clean up the trash there（尾句，列舉 3）.                                          【w學測】
1 What was the first piece of man-made space trash? !強調字詞
   A A camera.            B A tool kit.           C A fuel tank.        D A broken satellite.
2 Why were scientists NOT concerned about space trash in the beginning? !因果關係
   A It did not cause any problem.
   B It no longer served any useful purpose.
   C It was millions of miles away from the Earth.
   D It was regarded as similar to comets and stars.
3 What has been done about the space trash problem? !列舉用法
   A Scientists have cleaned up most of the trash.
   B Large pieces of space trash are being closely watched.
   C Many nations have worked together to stop polluting space.
   D Ground stations are built to help properly store the trash in space.
                                                                            1D     2A     3B




    同學研究以上重點，一定知道能有效整理文章，才能穩拿滿分。請根據以上所學
    的重點，將會考的句子、部分劃底線、圈出。
       Coal remains a critical component of the world’s energy supply despite its image
  as a polluter. As an enemy of environmentalists, it creates much pollution. In this case,
  coal still has the undeniable advantages of being available and easy to ship and burn.
  Besides, the biggest attraction is low cost. In addition to demand for thermal coal, which
  is burned in power plants, use of metallurgical coal（冶金煤） is also expected to double.
  For the time being, coal seems to be avoiding a serious potential impediment to its use:
  international treaties restricting greenhouse gas emissions. So far, such agreements to
  prevent climate change have been ineffective.

翻譯占分 8 分。共 2 題，每題 4 分。每一題可能測驗三個高中英文「詞彙」、一至二個
高中英文「片語」、一組高中英文「文法句型（請讀附贈的《速讀手冊》）」。在這個
單元，我們先了解學測方向，並複習最常考的重點：
1 熟悉重要句型。同學複習句型時，一定要練習翻譯。
2 用心於熱門議題的詞彙、片語。過去考過的議題有臺灣特色、社會變遷、高房價、
     國片、夜市、核能安全、少子化、糧食危機等。未來則有可能考海洋生態、媒體、
     健康飲食、人際互動、壓力處理、產業變化、通貨膨脹等議題。
3 練習翻譯時，務必養成謹慎處理「時態」的習慣。
4 103 年學測的翻譯題為經典考題，示範答題如下：
     1 有些年輕人辭掉都市裡的高薪工作，返回家鄉種植有機蔬菜。
      Some young people have quit∕resigned their well-paid∕high-paying jobs in the cities
      and gone back∕returned to their hometowns to grow∕cultivate organic vegetables.
     2 藉由決心與努力，很多人成功了，不但獲利更多，還過著更健康的生活。
       With determination and effort(s)∕hard work, many of them have succeeded; they have
       not only made more profits but (also) lived∕led a healthier life.

4 以 105 年學測的作文（引導寫作，107 年學測亦考此題型）為例，示範寫作如下：
   提示︰
   你認為家裡生活環境的維持應該是誰的責任呢？請寫一篇短文說明你的看法。文分
   兩段，第一段說明你對家事該如何分工的看法及理由；第二段舉例說明你家中家事
   分工的情形，並描述你自己做家事的經驗及感想。
    Cleaning a house is the business of every member in a family（主題句，直接破題）.
As far as the division of household chores is concerned, it is advisable that parents, together
with their children, make a list spelling out what needs doing so that every task can be
completed（句型多元）. Next, with a view to preventing friction, the whole family should
share the chores and reach a clear consensus on frequency and standards. Parents should
allow their children to choose the chores they prefer and, if necessary, assign tasks to them
（善用副詞結構）. Most importantly, parents must encourage their children to pitch in by
rewarding them properly. Giving praise or even an allowance may be a good way. By sharing
housework effectively, parents can feel proud of their children, and children can cultivate a
growing sense of responsibility（結論句，涵蓋整段重點）.
     In my family, my elder brother and I have shared household chores since we were kids
（主題句，直接破題）. Convinced that doing chores helps develop a child’s character,
my parents encourage us to share housework actively（分詞構句）. I remember that I was
responsible for putting away my toys when I was little. As I grow older, I can do more chores.
I have benef ited greatly from sharing housework. For example（舉例）, I have become
efficient and responsible . In order to finish chores and do my homework, I have learned to
arrange things in order of priority. Besides（承轉語詞）, while cleaning my house, I reflect
on what I do or say. Because of self-ref lection, I can aroid making the same mistakes and
become a desirable person. In a nutshell, it is through doing household chores that I not only
help keep my house clean but also better myself at a time（結論句，涵蓋整段重點；強調
句）.




  1 這一波經濟不景氣已經造成一些公司破產。


  2 除了簡樸生活與努力工作，我們也要保持樂觀，這樣我們才能度過危機。

二、綜合測驗（每題 6 分，共 30 分）
      French psychologist Alfred Binet (1859-1911) took a different approach from most
other psychologists of his day: He was interested in the workings of the       1     mind rather
than the nature of mental illness. He wanted to find a way to measure the ability to think and
reason, apart from education in any particular field. In 1905, he developed a test in which
he    2     children do tasks, such as follow commands, copy patterns, name objects, and put
things in order or arrange them properly. He later created a standard of measuring children’s
intelligence    3      the data he had collected from the French children he studied. If 70
percent of 8-year-olds could pass a particular test, then      4    in the test represented an
8-year-old’s level of intelligence. From Binet’s work, the phrase “intelligence quotient” (“IQ”)
entered the English vocabulary. The IQ is the ratio of “mental age” to chronological age times
100, with 100     5     the average. So, an 8-year-old who passes a 10-year-old’s test has an
IQ of 10 / 8 times 100, or 125.                                                     【w學測】

3 A composed of                                  B based on
     C resulting in                              D fighting against
4 A success                 B objection          C agreement            D discovery
5 A is                      B are                C been                 D being

                                 122
本講義使用方法：
先自行寫完中翻英，再翻到後面解答區對答案並確實訂正，若不知道自己寫的
對不對，可請老師幫忙，這樣才能真正進步。


若不是應屆考生，可把此本講義當作打好基礎的工具書
若是應屆考生，請依剩下考試的天數去計算一天差不多需要練習幾題


中翻英雖然重要，但作文占的分數仍然較多，作文注重的無非是內容完整度、
文法是否正確、用字措辭是否得宜、連接詞等等。不過我認為透過練中翻英題
型的過程中仍然會增進自己寫作的能力，像是學會多種連接詞的替換用法或是
十分實用的形容詞。


我在解答區都有將重要的單字以及連接詞作補充，希望能夠幫助到大家。接近
後面的部分我只有將比較難或是需要注意的字畫重點，如果有不會的可以自己
去查，剛好在查的同時能夠加深印象。


另外我認為可以將自己練習錯的部分寫在另外一本筆記本上，再自行將認為重
要的部分一併抄上去，在考前只要看那本錯題本我認為就足夠了，不用再花時
間一頁一頁翻。


像是選擇題之類的也可以使用此方法，將錯的或是覺得很重要的抄在一本筆記
本上，考前只要專注複習那本即可，也不會遇到在考前覺得很多東西但不知道
要讀什麼的窘境。


試題解答僅供參考，一定有其他種的寫法。因為是人工打字的關係，所以一定
會多少有錯誤，請見諒。


P2-10 學測指考歷屆試題
P10-19 其餘試題
P20-37 解答區

最後祝大家考試順利！
歷屆

93
雖然 Lily 生來又瞎又聾，但她從不氣餒。




她的故事證明了，我們只要努力必能成功。




科技讓我們的生活更舒適，然而它也被利用來犯罪。




根據最新的新聞報導，最常見的例子是網際網路詐財。




94
人類對外太空所知非常有限，但長久以來我們對它卻很感興趣。




太空科技的快速發展，使我們得以探索它的奧秘。




身為地球村的成員，我們不應該把自己侷限在這個小島上。




我們不但應該參與國際性的活動，並且應該展現我們自己的文化特色。
95
一般人都知道閱讀對孩子有益。




老師應該多鼓勵學生到圖書館借書。




為提供一個無煙的用餐環境，許多餐廳不允許室內抽煙。




雖然遭到許多癮君子的反對，這對不抽煙的人的確是一大福音。




96
如果我們只為自己而活，就不會真正地感到快樂。
